        "prep_coeff": prep_coeff,
        "pattern_data": pattern_data,
        "patterns_by_prk": patterns_by_prk,
    }
    return instance


def build_model(instance, time_limit):
    config = instance["config"]
    periods = instance["periods"]
    relays = instance["relays"]
    payloads = instance["payloads"]
    aircraft = instance["aircraft"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    districts = instance["districts"]
    relay_data = instance["relay_data"]
    aircraft_data = instance["aircraft_data"]
    payload_stock = instance["payload_stock"]
    period_capacity = instance["period_capacity"]
    aircraft_period_capacity = instance["aircraft_period_capacity"]
    district_requirements = instance["district_requirements"]
    district_period_targets = instance["district_period_targets"]
    relays_in_district = instance["relays_in_district"]
    trip_arcs = instance["trip_arcs"]
    arcs_by_prk = instance["arcs_by_prk"]
    arcs_by_period = instance["arcs_by_period"]
    arcs_by_aircraft = instance["arcs_by_aircraft"]
    arcs_by_period_aircraft = instance["arcs_by_period_aircraft"]
    arcs_by_payload = instance["arcs_by_payload"]
    fuel_coeff = instance["fuel_coeff"]
    prep_coeff = instance["prep_coeff"]
    pattern_data = instance["pattern_data"]
    patterns_by_prk = instance["patterns_by_prk"]
    scenario_data = instance["scenario_data"]

    required_restored = int(config["required_restored_relays"])

    model = Model("cascadia_relay_drops")
    model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)

    x_vars = {}
    for key in trip_arcs:
        period, aircraft_id, relay, payload = key
        ub = min(
            relay_data[relay]["max_total_drops"],
            payload_stock[payload],
            period_capacity[period]["max_trips"],
            aircraft_data[aircraft_id]["max_total_trips"],
            aircraft_period_capacity[(period, aircraft_id)]["max_trips"],
            instance["relay_period_access"][(period, relay)]["max_period_drops"],
        )
        x_vars[key] = model.addVar(vtype="INTEGER", lb=0, ub=ub, name=f"x[{period},{aircraft_id},{relay},{payload}]")

    lambda_vars = {}
    for relay in relays:
        for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]:
            lambda_vars[pattern_key] = model.addVar(vtype="BINARY", name=f"lam[{relay},{pattern_key[1]}]")
            model.chgVarBranchPriority(lambda_vars[pattern_key], 10)

    alpha_vars = {}
    u_vars = {}
    n_relays = len(relays)

    for scenario in scenarios:
        alpha_vars[(scenario, 0, 0)] = model.addVar(lb=1.0, ub=1.0, name=f"alpha[{scenario},0,0]")
        for m in range(1, required_restored + 1):
            alpha_vars[(scenario, 0, m)] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=0.0, name=f"alpha[{scenario},0,{m}]")

        for i in range(1, n_relays + 1):
            prev_max = min(i - 1, required_restored)
            curr_max = min(i, required_restored)
            relay = relays[i - 1]
            patterns = pattern_data[relay]

            for m in range(curr_max + 1):
                alpha_vars[(scenario, i, m)] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=1.0, name=f"alpha[{scenario},{i},{m}]")

            for pattern_key in patterns:
                for m in range(prev_max + 1):
                    u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, m)] = model.addVar(
                        lb=0.0,
                        ub=1.0,
                        name=f"u[{scenario},{i},{pattern_key[1]},{m}]",
                    )

    mission_prob_vars = {
        scenario: model.addVar(lb=0.0, ub=1.0, name=f"mission[{scenario}]")
        for scenario in scenarios
    }

    for relay in relays:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(lambda_vars[pattern_key] for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]) == 1,
            name=f"one_pattern[{relay}]",
        )

    for relay in relays:
        for period in periods:
            for payload in payloads:
                pattern_terms = patterns_by_prk.get((relay, period, payload), [])
                if not pattern_terms and not arcs_by_prk.get((period, relay, payload)):
                    continue
                model.addCons(
                    quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_prk.get((period, relay, payload), []))
                    == quicksum(amount * lambda_vars[pattern_key] for pattern_key, amount in pattern_terms),
                    name=f"link[{relay},{period},{payload}]",
                )

    for payload in payloads:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_payload[payload]) <= payload_stock[payload],
            name=f"payload_stock[{payload}]",
        )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(fuel_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in trip_arcs)
        <= instance["operations_budget"]["fuel_liters"],
        name="fuel_budget",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(prep_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in trip_arcs)
        <= instance["operations_budget"]["prep_hours"],
        name="prep_budget",
    )

    for period in periods:
        cap = period_capacity[period]
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_period[period]) <= cap["max_trips"],
            name=f"period_trips[{period}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(fuel_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_period[period]) <= cap["fuel_liters"],
            name=f"period_fuel[{period}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(prep_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_period[period]) <= cap["prep_hours"],
            name=f"period_prep[{period}]",
        )

    for aircraft_id in aircraft:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_aircraft[aircraft_id])
            <= aircraft_data[aircraft_id]["max_total_trips"],
            name=f"aircraft_trips[{aircraft_id}]",
        )

    for period in periods:
        for aircraft_id in aircraft:
            cap = aircraft_period_capacity[(period, aircraft_id)]
            keys = arcs_by_period_aircraft.get((period, aircraft_id), [])
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in keys) <= cap["max_trips"],
                name=f"ap_trips[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(fuel_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in keys) <= cap["max_fuel_liters"],
                name=f"ap_fuel[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(prep_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in keys) <= cap["max_prep_hours"],
                name=f"ap_prep[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
            )

    for district in districts:
        district_relays = relays_in_district[district]
        req = district_requirements[district]
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern_data[pattern_key]["total_drops"] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                for relay in district_relays
                for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
            )
            >= req["min_total_drops"],
            name=f"district_drops[{district}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern_data[pattern_key]["active"] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                for relay in district_relays
                for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
            )
            >= req["min_active_relays"],
            name=f"district_active[{district}]",
        )

    for period in periods:
        for district in districts:
            target = district_period_targets[(period, district)]
            district_relays = relays_in_district[district]
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(
                    pattern_data[pattern_key]["cumulative_drops"][period] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                    for relay in district_relays
                    for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
                )
                >= target["min_cumulative_drops"],
                name=f"district_cum_drops[{period},{district}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(
                    pattern_data[pattern_key]["cumulative_active"][period] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                    for relay in district_relays
                    for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
                )
                >= target["min_cumulative_active_relays"],
                name=f"district_cum_active[{period},{district}]",
            )

    for scenario in scenarios:
        for i in range(1, n_relays + 1):
            relay = relays[i - 1]
            patterns = pattern_data[relay]
            prev_max = min(i - 1, required_restored)
            curr_max = min(i, required_restored)

            for pattern_key in patterns:
                lam = lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                for m in range(prev_max + 1):
                    u = u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, m)]
                    alpha_prev = alpha_vars[(scenario, i - 1, m)]
                    model.addCons(u <= lam, name=f"u_lam[{scenario},{i},{pattern_key[1]},{m}]")
                    model.addCons(u <= alpha_prev, name=f"u_alpha[{scenario},{i},{pattern_key[1]},{m}]")
                    model.addCons(
                        u >= alpha_prev - (1.0 - lam),
                        name=f"u_lb[{scenario},{i},{pattern_key[1]},{m}]",
                    )

            for m in range(prev_max + 1):
                model.addCons(
                    quicksum(u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, m)] for pattern_key in patterns)
                    == alpha_vars[(scenario, i - 1, m)],
                    name=f"u_partition[{scenario},{i},{m}]",
                )

            for m in range(curr_max + 1):
                if m < required_restored:
                    expr = quicksum(
                        (1.0 - pattern_data[pattern_key]["restore_prob"][scenario])
                        * u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, m)]
                        for pattern_key in patterns
                        if m <= prev_max
                    )
                    if m > 0:
                        expr += quicksum(
                            pattern_data[pattern_key]["restore_prob"][scenario]
                            * u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, m - 1)]
                            for pattern_key in patterns
                            if m - 1 <= prev_max
                        )
                    model.addCons(
                        alpha_vars[(scenario, i, m)] == expr,
                        name=f"alpha_rec[{scenario},{i},{m}]",
                    )
                else:
                    expr = quicksum(
                        u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, required_restored)]
                        for pattern_key in patterns
                        if prev_max >= required_restored
                    )
                    expr += quicksum(
                        pattern_data[pattern_key]["restore_prob"][scenario]
                        * u_vars[(scenario, i, pattern_key, required_restored - 1)]
                        for pattern_key in patterns
                        if required_restored - 1 <= prev_max
                    )
                    model.addCons(
                        alpha_vars[(scenario, i, required_restored)] == expr,
                        name=f"alpha_cap[{scenario},{i}]",
                    )

        model.addCons(
            mission_prob_vars[scenario] == alpha_vars[(scenario, n_relays, required_restored)],
            name=f"mission_link[{scenario}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            mission_prob_vars[scenario] >= scenario_data[scenario]["min_mission_probability"],
            name=f"mission_min[{scenario}]",
        )

    model.setObjective(
        quicksum(
            scenario_data[scenario]["scenario_probability"] * mission_prob_vars[scenario]
            for scenario in scenarios
        ),
        "maximize",
    )

    return model, x_vars, lambda_vars, mission_prob_vars


def build_surrogate_model(instance, time_limit):
    periods = instance["periods"]
    relays = instance["relays"]
    payloads = instance["payloads"]
    aircraft = instance["aircraft"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    relay_data = instance["relay_data"]
    aircraft_data = instance["aircraft_data"]
    payload_stock = instance["payload_stock"]
    period_capacity = instance["period_capacity"]
    aircraft_period_capacity = instance["aircraft_period_capacity"]
    district_requirements = instance["district_requirements"]
    district_period_targets = instance["district_period_targets"]
    relays_in_district = instance["relays_in_district"]
    trip_arcs = instance["trip_arcs"]
    arcs_by_prk = instance["arcs_by_prk"]
    arcs_by_period = instance["arcs_by_period"]
    arcs_by_aircraft = instance["arcs_by_aircraft"]
    arcs_by_period_aircraft = instance["arcs_by_period_aircraft"]
    arcs_by_payload = instance["arcs_by_payload"]
    fuel_coeff = instance["fuel_coeff"]
    prep_coeff = instance["prep_coeff"]
    pattern_data = instance["pattern_data"]
    patterns_by_prk = instance["patterns_by_prk"]
    scenario_data = instance["scenario_data"]

    model = Model("cascadia_relay_drops_surrogate")
    model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
    model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)

    x_vars = {}
    for key in trip_arcs:
        period, aircraft_id, relay, payload = key
        ub = min(
            relay_data[relay]["max_total_drops"],
            payload_stock[payload],
            period_capacity[period]["max_trips"],
            aircraft_data[aircraft_id]["max_total_trips"],
            aircraft_period_capacity[(period, aircraft_id)]["max_trips"],
            instance["relay_period_access"][(period, relay)]["max_period_drops"],
        )
        x_vars[key] = model.addVar(vtype="INTEGER", lb=0, ub=ub, name=f"x[{period},{aircraft_id},{relay},{payload}]")

    lambda_vars = {}
    for relay in relays:
        for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]:
            lambda_vars[pattern_key] = model.addVar(vtype="BINARY", name=f"lam[{relay},{pattern_key[1]}]")
            model.chgVarBranchPriority(lambda_vars[pattern_key], 10)

    for relay in relays:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(lambda_vars[pattern_key] for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]) == 1,
            name=f"one_pattern[{relay}]",
        )

    for relay in relays:
        for period in periods:
            for payload in payloads:
                pattern_terms = patterns_by_prk.get((relay, period, payload), [])
                if not pattern_terms and not arcs_by_prk.get((period, relay, payload)):
                    continue
                model.addCons(
                    quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_prk.get((period, relay, payload), []))
                    == quicksum(amount * lambda_vars[pattern_key] for pattern_key, amount in pattern_terms),
                    name=f"link[{relay},{period},{payload}]",
                )

    for payload in payloads:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_payload[payload]) <= payload_stock[payload],
            name=f"payload_stock[{payload}]",
        )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(fuel_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in trip_arcs)
        <= instance["operations_budget"]["fuel_liters"],
        name="fuel_budget",
    )
    model.addCons(
        quicksum(prep_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in trip_arcs)
        <= instance["operations_budget"]["prep_hours"],
        name="prep_budget",
    )

    for period in periods:
        cap = period_capacity[period]
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_period[period]) <= cap["max_trips"],
            name=f"period_trips[{period}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(fuel_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_period[period]) <= cap["fuel_liters"],
            name=f"period_fuel[{period}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(prep_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_period[period]) <= cap["prep_hours"],
            name=f"period_prep[{period}]",
        )

    for aircraft_id in aircraft:
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in arcs_by_aircraft[aircraft_id])
            <= aircraft_data[aircraft_id]["max_total_trips"],
            name=f"aircraft_trips[{aircraft_id}]",
        )

    for period in periods:
        for aircraft_id in aircraft:
            cap = aircraft_period_capacity[(period, aircraft_id)]
            keys = arcs_by_period_aircraft.get((period, aircraft_id), [])
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(x_vars[key] for key in keys) <= cap["max_trips"],
                name=f"ap_trips[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(fuel_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in keys) <= cap["max_fuel_liters"],
                name=f"ap_fuel[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(prep_coeff[key] * x_vars[key] for key in keys) <= cap["max_prep_hours"],
                name=f"ap_prep[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
            )

    for district in instance["districts"]:
        district_relays = relays_in_district[district]
        req = district_requirements[district]
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern_data[pattern_key]["total_drops"] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                for relay in district_relays
                for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
            )
            >= req["min_total_drops"],
            name=f"district_drops[{district}]",
        )
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(
                pattern_data[pattern_key]["active"] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                for relay in district_relays
                for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
            )
            >= req["min_active_relays"],
            name=f"district_active[{district}]",
        )

    for period in periods:
        for district in instance["districts"]:
            target = district_period_targets[(period, district)]
            district_relays = relays_in_district[district]
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(
                    pattern_data[pattern_key]["cumulative_drops"][period] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                    for relay in district_relays
                    for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
                )
                >= target["min_cumulative_drops"],
                name=f"district_cum_drops[{period},{district}]",
tool19. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '760,1220p' /app/submissions/solve.py"
result20. Command output
                name=f"district_cum_drops[{period},{district}]",
            )
            model.addCons(
                quicksum(
                    pattern_data[pattern_key]["cumulative_active"][period] * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
                    for relay in district_relays
                    for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
                )
                >= target["min_cumulative_active_relays"],
                name=f"district_cum_active[{period},{district}]",
            )

    model.setObjective(
        quicksum(
            scenario_data[scenario]["scenario_probability"]
            * pattern_data[pattern_key]["restore_prob"][scenario]
            * lambda_vars[pattern_key]
            for relay in relays
            for pattern_key in pattern_data[relay]
            for scenario in scenarios
        ),
        "maximize",
    )

    return model, x_vars, lambda_vars


def solve_model(instance):
    env_limit = os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS")
    if env_limit:
        try:
            hard_limit = min(300.0, float(env_limit))
        except ValueError:
            hard_limit = 300.0
    else:
        hard_limit = 300.0

    time_limit = min(hard_limit, 60.0)

    model, x_vars, lambda_vars = build_surrogate_model(instance, time_limit)
    model.optimize()

    solution = None
    if model.getNSols() > 0:
        solution = model.getBestSol()

    result = {
        "model": model,
        "x_vars": x_vars,
        "lambda_vars": lambda_vars,
        "time_limit": time_limit,
        "hard_limit": hard_limit,
        "has_solution": solution is not None,
        "best_solution": solution,
        "status": str(model.getStatus()),
        "n_solutions": int(model.getNSols()),
        "n_vars": int(model.getNVars()),
        "n_conss": int(model.getNConss()),
        "solve_time": float(model.getSolvingTime()),
        "search_model": "surrogate_weighted_relay_restoration",
    }
    if solution is not None:
        result["objective"] = float(model.getObjVal())
        result["best_bound"] = float(model.getDualbound())
        result["gap"] = float(model.getGap())
    else:
        result["objective"] = None
        result["best_bound"] = float(model.getDualbound())
        result["gap"] = None
    return result


def export_solution(instance, result):
    if not result["has_solution"]:
        raise RuntimeError("SCIP did not produce an incumbent solution.")

    model = result["model"]
    solution = result["best_solution"]
    rows = []
    for key, var in result["x_vars"].items():
        value = model.getSolVal(solution, var)
        if value > 1e-8:
            trips = int(round(value))
            rows.append(
                {
                    "variable": "drop",
                    "period": key[0],
                    "aircraft": key[1],
                    "relay": key[2],
                    "payload_type": key[3],
                    "trips": str(trips),
                }
            )

    rows.sort(key=lambda row: (row["period"], row["aircraft"], row["relay"], row["payload_type"]))

    with open(SOLUTION_PATH, "w", newline="") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=instance["config"]["solution_columns"])
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def normalized_violation(lhs, sense, rhs):
    scale = max(1.0, abs(rhs))
    if sense == "<=":
        violation = max(0.0, lhs - rhs)
    elif sense == ">=":
        violation = max(0.0, rhs - lhs)
    elif sense == "==":
        violation = abs(lhs - rhs)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown sense {sense}")
    return violation, violation / scale


def compute_mission_probability(relay_success_prob, required_restored):
    state = [0.0] * (required_restored + 1)
    state[0] = 1.0
    for prob in relay_success_prob:
        new_state = [0.0] * (required_restored + 1)
        new_state[0] = state[0] * (1.0 - prob)
        for m in range(1, required_restored):
            new_state[m] = state[m] * (1.0 - prob) + state[m - 1] * prob
        new_state[required_restored] = state[required_restored] + state[required_restored - 1] * prob
        state = new_state
    return state[required_restored]


def audit_solution(instance, solution_path):
    config = instance["config"]
    required_columns = config["solution_columns"]
    required_restored = int(config["required_restored_relays"])
    periods = instance["periods"]
    relays = instance["relays"]
    payloads = instance["payloads"]
    aircraft = instance["aircraft"]
    scenarios = instance["scenarios"]
    relay_data = instance["relay_data"]
    aircraft_data = instance["aircraft_data"]
    payload_stock = instance["payload_stock"]
    period_capacity = instance["period_capacity"]
    aircraft_period_capacity = instance["aircraft_period_capacity"]
    district_requirements = instance["district_requirements"]
    district_period_targets = instance["district_period_targets"]
    relays_in_district = instance["relays_in_district"]
    relay_period_access = instance["relay_period_access"]
    relay_payload_eligibility = instance["relay_payload_eligibility"]
    compat = instance["compat"]
    scenario_data = instance["scenario_data"]
    relay_payload_effectiveness = instance["relay_payload_effectiveness"]
    scenario_period_relay_effects = instance["scenario_period_relay_effects"]
    fuel_coeff = instance["fuel_coeff"]
    prep_coeff = instance["prep_coeff"]

    with open(solution_path, newline="") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        header = reader.fieldnames
        rows = list(reader)

    schema_ok = header == required_columns
    trip_values = {}
    group_residuals = []
    violated_groups = []

    def record(group, lhs, sense, rhs):
        violation, norm = normalized_violation(lhs, sense, rhs)
        group_residuals.append((group, violation, norm))
        if violation > 1e-7:
            violated_groups.append(group)

    integrality_residual = 0.0
    parse_errors = []
    for idx, row in enumerate(rows):
        key = (
            row.get("period", ""),
            row.get("aircraft", ""),
            row.get("relay", ""),
            row.get("payload_type", ""),
        )
        if row.get("variable") != "drop":
            parse_errors.append(f"row {idx + 2}: variable must be 'drop'")
            continue
        if key[0] not in periods or key[1] not in aircraft or key[2] not in relays or key[3] not in payloads:
            parse_errors.append(f"row {idx + 2}: invalid identifiers")
            continue
        try:
            trips_float = float(row.get("trips", ""))
        except ValueError:
            parse_errors.append(f"row {idx + 2}: trips is not numeric")
            continue
        rounded = round(trips_float)
        integrality_residual = max(integrality_residual, abs(trips_float - rounded))
        if abs(trips_float - rounded) > 1e-7 or rounded < 0:
            parse_errors.append(f"row {idx + 2}: trips must be a nonnegative integer")
            continue
        trip_values[key] = trip_values.get(key, 0) + int(rounded)

    for key, value in trip_values.items():
        period, aircraft_id, relay, payload = key
        if compat[(aircraft_id, payload)] == 0:
            parse_errors.append(f"incompatible aircraft/payload on {key}")
        if aircraft_period_capacity[(period, aircraft_id)]["available"] == 0:
            parse_errors.append(f"unavailable aircraft-period on {key}")
        if relay_period_access[(period, relay)]["accessible"] == 0:
            parse_errors.append(f"inaccessible relay-period on {key}")
        if relay_payload_eligibility[(relay, payload)] == 0:
            parse_errors.append(f"ineligible relay/payload on {key}")
        if (period, aircraft_id, relay, payload) not in fuel_coeff:
            parse_errors.append(f"unknown feasible arc on {key}")
        if value < 0:
            parse_errors.append(f"negative trips on {key}")

    if parse_errors:
        violated_groups.append("schema_or_domain")

    total_by_payload = defaultdict(int)
    total_by_period = defaultdict(int)
    fuel_by_period = defaultdict(float)
    prep_by_period = defaultdict(float)
    total_by_aircraft = defaultdict(int)
    total_by_period_aircraft = defaultdict(int)
    fuel_by_period_aircraft = defaultdict(float)
    prep_by_period_aircraft = defaultdict(float)
    total_by_relay = defaultdict(int)
    total_by_period_relay = defaultdict(int)
    total_by_prk = defaultdict(int)
    total_fuel = 0.0
    total_prep = 0.0

    for key, value in trip_values.items():
        period, aircraft_id, relay, payload = key
        total_by_payload[payload] += value
        total_by_period[period] += value
        total_by_aircraft[aircraft_id] += value
        total_by_period_aircraft[(period, aircraft_id)] += value
        total_by_relay[relay] += value
        total_by_period_relay[(period, relay)] += value
        total_by_prk[(period, relay, payload)] += value

        arc_key = (period, aircraft_id, relay, payload)
        arc_fuel = fuel_coeff.get(arc_key, 0.0) * value
        arc_prep = prep_coeff.get(arc_key, 0.0) * value
        total_fuel += arc_fuel
        total_prep += arc_prep
        fuel_by_period[period] += arc_fuel
        prep_by_period[period] += arc_prep
        fuel_by_period_aircraft[(period, aircraft_id)] += arc_fuel
        prep_by_period_aircraft[(period, aircraft_id)] += arc_prep

    for payload in payloads:
        record(f"payload_stock[{payload}]", total_by_payload[payload], "<=", payload_stock[payload])

    record("fuel_budget", total_fuel, "<=", instance["operations_budget"]["fuel_liters"])
    record("prep_budget", total_prep, "<=", instance["operations_budget"]["prep_hours"])

    for period in periods:
        cap = period_capacity[period]
        record(f"period_trips[{period}]", total_by_period[period], "<=", cap["max_trips"])
        record(f"period_fuel[{period}]", fuel_by_period[period], "<=", cap["fuel_liters"])
        record(f"period_prep[{period}]", prep_by_period[period], "<=", cap["prep_hours"])

    for aircraft_id in aircraft:
        record(
            f"aircraft_trips[{aircraft_id}]",
            total_by_aircraft[aircraft_id],
            "<=",
            aircraft_data[aircraft_id]["max_total_trips"],
        )

    for period in periods:
        for aircraft_id in aircraft:
            cap = aircraft_period_capacity[(period, aircraft_id)]
            record(
                f"ap_trips[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
                total_by_period_aircraft[(period, aircraft_id)],
                "<=",
                cap["max_trips"],
            )
            record(
                f"ap_fuel[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
                fuel_by_period_aircraft[(period, aircraft_id)],
                "<=",
                cap["max_fuel_liters"],
            )
            record(
                f"ap_prep[{period},{aircraft_id}]",
                prep_by_period_aircraft[(period, aircraft_id)],
                "<=",
                cap["max_prep_hours"],
            )

    for relay in relays:
        record(
            f"relay_total[{relay}]",
            total_by_relay[relay],
            "<=",
            relay_data[relay]["max_total_drops"],
        )
        for period in periods:
            record(
                f"relay_period[{period},{relay}]",
                total_by_period_relay[(period, relay)],
                "<=",
                relay_period_access[(period, relay)]["max_period_drops"],
            )

    relay_cum_period = defaultdict(dict)
    for relay in relays:
        running = 0
        for period in periods:
            running += total_by_period_relay[(period, relay)]
            relay_cum_period[relay][period] = running

    district_total_drops = defaultdict(int)
    district_active_relays = defaultdict(int)
    district_cum_drops = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))
    district_cum_active = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))

    for district, district_relays in relays_in_district.items():
        for relay in district_relays:
            district_total_drops[district] += total_by_relay[relay]
            district_active_relays[district] += 1 if total_by_relay[relay] > 0 else 0
            for period in periods:
                district_cum_drops[district][period] += relay_cum_period[relay][period]
                district_cum_active[district][period] += 1 if relay_cum_period[relay][period] > 0 else 0

    for district, req in district_requirements.items():
        record(f"district_drops[{district}]", district_total_drops[district], ">=", req["min_total_drops"])
        record(
            f"district_active[{district}]",
            district_active_relays[district],
            ">=",
            req["min_active_relays"],
        )

    for period in periods:
        for district in district_requirements:
            target = district_period_targets[(period, district)]
            record(
                f"district_cum_drops[{period},{district}]",
                district_cum_drops[district][period],
                ">=",
                target["min_cumulative_drops"],
            )
            record(
                f"district_cum_active[{period},{district}]",
                district_cum_active[district][period],
                ">=",
                target["min_cumulative_active_relays"],
            )

    relay_success = {scenario: {} for scenario in scenarios}
    mission_prob = {}
    for scenario in scenarios:
        probs = []
        for relay in relays:
            survival = 1.0
            for period in periods:
                for payload in payloads:
                    amount = total_by_prk[(period, relay, payload)]
                    if amount == 0:
                        continue
                    rho = (
                        relay_payload_effectiveness[(relay, payload)]
                        * relay_period_access[(period, relay)]["effectiveness_multiplier"]
                        * scenario_period_relay_effects[(scenario, period, relay)]
                    )
                    rho = max(0.0, min(1.0, rho))
                    survival *= (1.0 - rho) ** amount
                relay_success[scenario][relay] = 1.0 - survival
            probs.append(relay_success[scenario].get(relay, 0.0))
        mission_prob[scenario] = compute_mission_probability(probs, required_restored)
        record(
            f"mission_min[{scenario}]",
            mission_prob[scenario],
            ">=",
            scenario_data[scenario]["min_mission_probability"],
        )

    weighted_objective = sum(
        scenario_data[scenario]["scenario_probability"] * mission_prob[scenario]
        for scenario in scenarios
    )

    max_norm = max((norm for _, _, norm in group_residuals), default=0.0)
    violation_count = sum(1 for _, violation, _ in group_residuals if violation > 1e-7)
    if parse_errors:
        violation_count += 1

    active_relays = sum(1 for relay in relays if total_by_relay[relay] > 0)
    state_vector = {
        "artifact_present": solution_path.exists(),
        "schema_ok": schema_ok,
        "incumbent_trip_rows": len(rows),
        "max_normalized_residual": max_norm,
        "violated_constraint_groups": violation_count,
        "integrality_residual": integrality_residual,
        "weighted_objective": weighted_objective,
        "active_relays": active_relays,
        "served_districts": sum(1 for district in district_requirements if district_active_relays[district] > 0),
    }

    return {
        "schema_ok": schema_ok,
        "parse_errors": parse_errors,
        "group_residuals": group_residuals,
        "violated_groups": sorted(set(violated_groups)),
        "integrality_residual": integrality_residual,
        "mission_prob": mission_prob,
        "weighted_objective": weighted_objective,
        "state_vector": state_vector,
        "trip_values": trip_values,
    }


def format_float(value):
    if value is None:
        return "NA"
    return f"{value:.8f}"


def write_log(result, audit, start_time):

from pathlib import Path
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, build_model
inst = load_instance()
audit = audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
inc_obj = audit['weighted_objective']
trip_values = audit['trip_values']
pattern_data = inst['pattern_data']
relays = inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']
chosen = {}
for r in relays:
    counts = {(p,k): sum(trip_values.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts = {k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:
        if pattern_data[pk]['counts'] == counts:
            chosen[r] = pk
            break
print('inc', inc_obj)
for k in [1,2,3]:
    model,xv,lv,mp = build_model(inst, 30)
    model.addCons(sum(1 - lv[chosen[r]] for r in relays) <= k, name=f'local_branch_{k}')
    # incumbent objective cut
    model.addCons(sum(inst['scenario_data'][s]['scenario_probability']*mp[s] for s in inst['scenarios']) >= inc_obj + 1e-8, name='improve')
    sol = model.createPartialSol()
    for key,var in xv.items():
        model.setSolVal(sol, var, trip_values.get(key, 0))
    for r in relays:
        for pk in pattern_data[r]:
            model.setSolVal(sol, lv[pk], 1 if pk == chosen[r] else 0)
    ok = model.addSol(sol, free=True)
    print('k',k,'start_ok',ok)
    model.optimize()
    print('status',model.getStatus(),'nsol',model.getNSols(),'time',model.getSolvingTime(), 'bound', model.getDualbound())
    if model.getNSols():
        print('obj', model.getObjVal(), 'gap', model.getGap())

from pathlib import Path
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, compute_mission_probability
inst = load_instance()
audit = audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
trip_values = audit['trip_values']
relays=inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']; scenarios=inst['scenarios']
pattern_data=inst['pattern_data']; scenario_data=inst['scenario_data']
chosen={}
for r in relays:
    counts={(p,k):sum(trip_values.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts={k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    chosen[r]=next(pk for pk in pattern_data[r] if pattern_data[pk]['counts']==counts)

def obj(sel):
    req=inst['config']['required_restored_relays']
    total=0.0
    for s in scenarios:
        probs=[pattern_data[sel[r]]['restore_prob'][s] for r in relays]
        mp=compute_mission_probability(probs, req)
        total += scenario_data[s]['scenario_probability']*mp
    return total
base=obj(chosen)
print('base',base)
for r in relays:
    cand=[]
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:
        if pk==chosen[r]:
            continue
        sel=dict(chosen); sel[r]=pk
        cand.append((obj(sel),pk,pattern_data[pk]['counts']))

The pure objective signal says there are potentially better relay-pattern choices, but most of the promising ones consume more heavy-kit capacity. I’m screening those candidates through an exact assignment-feasibility MIP now so I only keep moves that remain publicly feasible.

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, compute_mission_probability

inst = load_instance()
audit = audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
inc_trip = audit['trip_values']
inc_obj = audit['weighted_objective']
relays=inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']; scenarios=inst['scenarios']
pattern_data=inst['pattern_data']; scenario_data=inst['scenario_data']
relay_data=inst['relay_data']; payload_stock=inst['payload_stock']; aircraft_data=inst['aircraft_data']; period_capacity=inst['period_capacity']; apcap=inst['aircraft_period_capacity']; arcs_by_prk=inst['arcs_by_prk']; arcs_by_payload=inst['arcs_by_payload']; arcs_by_period=inst['arcs_by_period']; arcs_by_aircraft=inst['arcs_by_aircraft']; arcs_by_period_aircraft=inst['arcs_by_period_aircraft']; trip_arcs=inst['trip_arcs']; fuel=inst['fuel_coeff']; prep=inst['prep_coeff']; district_requirements=inst['district_requirements']; district_period_targets=inst['district_period_targets']; relays_in_district=inst['relays_in_district']

# incumbent patterns
chosen={}
agg={}
for r in relays:
    counts={(p,k):sum(inc_trip.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts={k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    chosen[r]=next(pk for pk in pattern_data[r] if pattern_data[pk]['counts']==counts)
    agg[r]=counts

req=inst['config']['required_restored_relays']
def exact_obj(sel):
    total=0.0
    mission={}
    for s in scenarios:
        probs=[pattern_data[sel[r]]['restore_prob'][s] for r in relays]
        mp=compute_mission_probability(probs, req)
        mission[s]=mp
        total += scenario_data[s]['scenario_probability']*mp
    return total, mission

def pattern_hard_ok(sel):
    # district requirements from patterns
    for d, reqs in district_requirements.items():
        drops=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['total_drops'] for r in relays_in_district[d])
        active=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['active'] for r in relays_in_district[d])
        if drops < reqs['min_total_drops'] or active < reqs['min_active_relays']:
            return False
    for p in periods:
        for d, rels in relays_in_district.items():
            drops=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['cumulative_drops'][p] for r in rels)
            active=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['cumulative_active'][p] for r in rels)
            tgt=district_period_targets[(p,d)]
            if drops < tgt['min_cumulative_drops'] or active < tgt['min_cumulative_active_relays']:
                return False
    _, mission=exact_obj(sel)
    for s in scenarios:
        if mission[s] + 1e-12 < scenario_data[s]['min_mission_probability']:
            return False
    return True

def solve_assignment(sel, tl=2.0):
    req_counts={(p,r,k): pattern_data[sel[r]]['counts'].get((p,k),0) for r in relays for p in periods for k in payloads}
    m=Model('assign')
    m.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
    m.setParam('limits/time',tl)
    x={}
    for key in trip_arcs:
        p,a,r,k=key
        ub=req_counts[(p,r,k)]
        if ub>0:
            x[key]=m.addVar(vtype='INTEGER', lb=0, ub=ub, name=f'x[{p},{a},{r},{k}]')
    for r in relays:
        for p in periods:
            for k in payloads:
                keys=[key for key in arcs_by_prk.get((p,r,k),[]) if key in x]
                if req_counts[(p,r,k)]==0:
                    continue
                m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)==req_counts[(p,r,k)])
    for k in payloads:
        m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in arcs_by_payload[k] if key in x)<=payload_stock[k])
    m.addCons(quicksum(fuel[key]*x[key] for key in x)<=inst['operations_budget']['fuel_liters'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(prep[key]*x[key] for key in x)<=inst['operations_budget']['prep_hours'])
    for p in periods:
        m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in arcs_by_period[p] if key in x)<=period_capacity[p]['max_trips'])
        m.addCons(quicksum(fuel[key]*x[key] for key in arcs_by_period[p] if key in x)<=period_capacity[p]['fuel_liters'])
        m.addCons(quicksum(prep[key]*x[key] for key in arcs_by_period[p] if key in x)<=period_capacity[p]['prep_hours'])
    for a in aircraft:
        m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in arcs_by_aircraft[a] if key in x)<=aircraft_data[a]['max_total_trips'])
    for p in periods:
        for a in aircraft:
            keys=[key for key in arcs_by_period_aircraft.get((p,a),[]) if key in x]
            cap=apcap[(p,a)]
            m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)<=cap['max_trips'])
            m.addCons(quicksum(fuel[key]*x[key] for key in keys)<=cap['max_fuel_liters'])
            m.addCons(quicksum(prep[key]*x[key] for key in keys)<=cap['max_prep_hours'])
    # minimize deviation from incumbent assignment on active arcs only
    dvars=[]
    for key,var in x.items():
        d=m.addVar(lb=0.0, name='d')
        inc=inc_trip.get(key,0)
        m.addCons(d >= var - inc)
        m.addCons(d >= inc - var)
        dvars.append(d)
    m.setObjective(quicksum(dvars), 'minimize')
    sol=m.createSol()
    for key,var in x.items():
        m.setSolVal(sol, var, inc_trip.get(key,0))
    m.addSol(sol, free=True)
    m.optimize()
    if m.getNSols()==0:
        return None
    s=m.getBestSol()
    vals={key:int(round(m.getSolVal(s,var))) for key,var in x.items() if m.getSolVal(s,var)>1e-8}
    return vals, float(m.getObjVal()), str(m.getStatus())

# top candidates by single relay move
cand=[]
for r in relays:
    base_sel=dict(chosen)
    vals=[]
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:
        if pk==chosen[r]:
            continue
        sel=dict(base_sel); sel[r]=pk
        obj, mission = exact_obj(sel)
        vals.append((obj,pk,mission))
    vals.sort(reverse=True,key=lambda t:t[0])
    for obj,pk,mission in vals[:12]:
        cand.append((obj,r,pk,mission))

cand.sort(reverse=True,key=lambda t:t[0])
print('testing',len(cand),'single-relay candidates, incumbent',inc_obj)
best=None
for obj,r,pk,mission in cand:
    if obj <= inc_obj + 1e-10:
        continue
    sel=dict(chosen); sel[r]=pk
    if not pattern_hard_ok(sel):
        continue
    assign=solve_assignment(sel, tl=2.0)
    if assign is None:
        continue
    vals, dev, status = assign
    print('feasible',r,pk,'obj',obj,'delta',obj-inc_obj,'dev',dev,'status',status)
    if best is None or obj > best[0]:
        best=(obj,r,pk,vals,mission,dev,status)
print('BEST',best[:4] if best else None)
PY"

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, compute_mission_probability, build_model
inst=load_instance(); audit=audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
inc_obj=audit['weighted_objective']; trip=audit['trip_values']
relays=inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']; scenarios=inst['scenarios']; pattern_data=inst['pattern_data']; scenario_data=inst['scenario_data']
chosen={}
for r in relays:
    counts={(p,k):sum(trip.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts={k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    chosen[r]=next(pk for pk in pattern_data[r] if pattern_data[pk]['counts']==counts)

def obj_sel(sel):
    req=inst['config']['required_restored_relays']
    total=0.0
    for s in scenarios:
        probs=[pattern_data[sel[r]]['restore_prob'][s] for r in relays]
        total += scenario_data[s]['scenario_probability']*compute_mission_probability(probs, req)
    return total

red=deepcopy(inst)
new_pd={}
new_pprk={}
for r in relays:
    scored=[]
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:
        sel=dict(chosen); sel[r]=pk
        scored.append((obj_sel(sel), pk))
    scored.sort(reverse=True, key=lambda x:x[0])
    keep={chosen[r]}
    for _,pk in scored[:8]:
        keep.add(pk)
    # also keep patterns closest in total drops and payload balance
    inc_counts=pattern_data[chosen[r]]['counts']
    dist=[]
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:
        c=pattern_data[pk]['counts']
        d=0
        keys=set(inc_counts)|set(c)
        for kk in keys:
            d += abs(inc_counts.get(kk,0)-c.get(kk,0))
        dist.append((d, pk))
    dist.sort(key=lambda x:(x[0],x[1][1]))
    for _,pk in dist[:8]:
        keep.add(pk)
    keep=sorted(keep,key=lambda x:x[1])
    new_pd[r]=keep
    for pk in keep:
        new_pd[pk]=pattern_data[pk]
        for (p,k),amt in pattern_data[pk]['counts'].items():
            new_pprk.setdefault((r,p,k),[]).append((pk,amt))
red['pattern_data']=new_pd
red['patterns_by_prk']=new_pprk
print('reduced patterns',sum(len(new_pd[r]) for r in relays))
for k in [1,2,3]:
    model,xv,lv,mp=build_model(red,60)
    model.addCons(sum(1-lv[chosen[r]] for r in relays) <= k)
    model.addCons(sum(red['scenario_data'][s]['scenario_probability']*mp[s] for s in red['scenarios']) >= inc_obj+1e-8)
    model.optimize()
    print('k',k,'status',model.getStatus(),'nsol',model.getNSols(),'time',model.getSolvingTime(),'bound',model.getDualbound())
    if model.getNSols():
        print('obj',model.getObjVal(),'gap',model.getGap())
PY"

from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, compute_mission_probability, build_model
inst=load_instance(); audit=audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
inc_obj=audit['weighted_objective']; trip=audit['trip_values']
relays=inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']; scenarios=inst['scenarios']; pattern_data=inst['pattern_data']; scenario_data=inst['scenario_data']
chosen={}
for r in relays:
    counts={(p,k):sum(trip.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts={k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    chosen[r]=next(pk for pk in pattern_data[r] if pattern_data[pk]['counts']==counts)

def obj_sel(sel):
    req=inst['config']['required_restored_relays']
    total=0.0
    for s in scenarios:
        probs=[pattern_data[sel[r]]['restore_prob'][s] for r in relays]
        total += scenario_data[s]['scenario_probability']*compute_mission_probability(probs, req)
    return total

    scored=[]
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:

    scored.sort(reverse=True, key=lambda x:x[0])

        keep.add(pk)

    model.addCons(sum(1-lv[chosen[r]] for r in relays) <= 2)
    model.addCons(sum(red['scenario_data'][s]['scenario_probability']*mp[s] for s in red['scenarios']) >= inc_obj + eps)
    model.optimize()
    print('eps',eps,'status',model.getStatus(),'nsol',model.getNSols(),'time',model.getSolvingTime(),'bound',model.getDualbound(), 'primal', (model.getObjVal() if model.getNSols() else None))

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
from itertools import combinations, product
from pathlib import Path
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, compute_mission_probability

inst = load_instance()
audit = audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
inc_trip = audit['trip_values']
inc_obj = audit['weighted_objective']
relays=inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']; scenarios=inst['scenarios']
pattern_data=inst['pattern_data']; scenario_data=inst['scenario_data']
relay_data=inst['relay_data']; payload_stock=inst['payload_stock']; aircraft_data=inst['aircraft_data']; period_capacity=inst['period_capacity']; apcap=inst['aircraft_period_capacity']; arcs_by_prk=inst['arcs_by_prk']; arcs_by_payload=inst['arcs_by_payload']; arcs_by_period=inst['arcs_by_period']; arcs_by_aircraft=inst['arcs_by_aircraft']; arcs_by_period_aircraft=inst['arcs_by_period_aircraft']; trip_arcs=inst['trip_arcs']; fuel=inst['fuel_coeff']; prep=inst['prep_coeff']; district_requirements=inst['district_requirements']; district_period_targets=inst['district_period_targets']; relays_in_district=inst['relays_in_district']

chosen={}
for r in relays:
    counts={(p,k):sum(inc_trip.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts={k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    chosen[r]=next(pk for pk in pattern_data[r] if pattern_data[pk]['counts']==counts)

req=inst['config']['required_restored_relays']
def exact_obj(sel):
    total=0.0
    mission={}
    for s in scenarios:
        probs=[pattern_data[sel[r]]['restore_prob'][s] for r in relays]
        mp=compute_mission_probability(probs, req)
        mission[s]=mp
        total += scenario_data[s]['scenario_probability']*mp
    return total, mission

def hard_pattern_ok(sel, mission=None):
    for d, reqs in district_requirements.items():
        drops=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['total_drops'] for r in relays_in_district[d])
        active=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['active'] for r in relays_in_district[d])
        if drops < reqs['min_total_drops'] or active < reqs['min_active_relays']:
            return False
    for p in periods:
        for d, rels in relays_in_district.items():
            drops=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['cumulative_drops'][p] for r in rels)
            active=sum(pattern_data[sel[r]]['cumulative_active'][p] for r in rels)
            tgt=district_period_targets[(p,d)]
            if drops < tgt['min_cumulative_drops'] or active < tgt['min_cumulative_active_relays']:
                return False
    if mission is None:
        _, mission = exact_obj(sel)
    for s in scenarios:
        if mission[s] + 1e-12 < scenario_data[s]['min_mission_probability']:
            return False
    return True

def solve_assignment(sel, tl=3.0):
    req_counts={(p,r,k): pattern_data[sel[r]]['counts'].get((p,k),0) for r in relays for p in periods for k in payloads}
    m=Model('assign')
    m.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
    m.setParam('limits/time',tl)
    x={}
    for key in trip_arcs:
        p,a,r,k=key
        ub=req_counts[(p,r,k)]
        if ub>0:
            x[key]=m.addVar(vtype='INTEGER', lb=0, ub=ub)
    for r in relays:
        for p in periods:
            for k in payloads:
                reqv=req_counts[(p,r,k)]
                if reqv==0:
                    continue
                keys=[key for key in arcs_by_prk.get((p,r,k),[]) if key in x]
                m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)==reqv)
    for k in payloads:
        keys=[key for key in arcs_by_payload[k] if key in x]
        m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)<=payload_stock[k])
    m.addCons(quicksum(fuel[key]*x[key] for key in x)<=inst['operations_budget']['fuel_liters'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(prep[key]*x[key] for key in x)<=inst['operations_budget']['prep_hours'])
    for p in periods:
        keys=[key for key in arcs_by_period[p] if key in x]
        m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)<=period_capacity[p]['max_trips'])
        m.addCons(quicksum(fuel[key]*x[key] for key in keys)<=period_capacity[p]['fuel_liters'])
        m.addCons(quicksum(prep[key]*x[key] for key in keys)<=period_capacity[p]['prep_hours'])
    for a in aircraft:
        keys=[key for key in arcs_by_aircraft[a] if key in x]
        m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)<=aircraft_data[a]['max_total_trips'])
    for p in periods:
        for a in aircraft:
            keys=[key for key in arcs_by_period_aircraft.get((p,a),[]) if key in x]
            cap=apcap[(p,a)]
            m.addCons(quicksum(x[key] for key in keys)<=cap['max_trips'])
            m.addCons(quicksum(fuel[key]*x[key] for key in keys)<=cap['max_fuel_liters'])
            m.addCons(quicksum(prep[key]*x[key] for key in keys)<=cap['max_prep_hours'])
    dvars=[]
    for key,var in x.items():
        d=m.addVar(lb=0.0)
        inc=inc_trip.get(key,0)
        m.addCons(d >= var - inc)
        m.addCons(d >= inc - var)
        dvars.append(d)
    m.setObjective(quicksum(dvars), 'minimize')
    sol=m.createSol()
    for key,var in x.items():
        m.setSolVal(sol, var, inc_trip.get(key,0))
    m.addSol(sol, free=True)
    m.optimize()
    if m.getNSols()==0:
        return None
    return True

# top alternatives per relay by exact objective in single-relay neighborhood
alt={}
for r in relays:
    arr=[]
    for pk in pattern_data[r]:
        sel=dict(chosen); sel[r]=pk
        obj, mission = exact_obj(sel)
        if obj > inc_obj + 1e-6:
            arr.append((obj,pk,mission))
    arr.sort(reverse=True,key=lambda t:t[0])
    alt[r]=[pk for _,pk,_ in arr[:6]]
    print(r, len(alt[r]))

pairs=[]
for r1,r2 in combinations(relays,2):
    for pk1,pk2 in product(alt[r1], alt[r2]):
        sel=dict(chosen); sel[r1]=pk1; sel[r2]=pk2
        obj, mission = exact_obj(sel)
        if obj > inc_obj + 1e-6 and hard_pattern_ok(sel, mission):
            pairs.append((obj,r1,pk1,r2,pk2,mission))
pairs.sort(reverse=True,key=lambda t:t[0])
print('pair candidates after pattern checks', len(pairs))
best=None
for item in pairs[:150]:
    obj,r1,pk1,r2,pk2,mission = item
    sel=dict(chosen); sel[r1]=pk1; sel[r2]=pk2
    feas=solve_assignment(sel, tl=3.0)
    if feas:
        print('feasible pair',obj-inc_obj,r1,pk1,r2,pk2)
        best=item
        break
print('best',best[:5] if best else None)
PY"

from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from submissions.solve import load_instance, audit_solution, build_model
inst=load_instance(); audit=audit_solution(inst, Path('/app/submissions/solution.csv'))
inc_obj=audit['weighted_objective']; trip=audit['trip_values']
relays=inst['relays']; periods=inst['periods']; payloads=inst['payloads']; aircraft=inst['aircraft']; pattern_data=inst['pattern_data']
chosen={}
for r in relays:
    counts={(p,k):sum(trip.get((p,a,r,k),0) for a in aircraft) for p in periods for k in payloads}
    counts={k:v for k,v in counts.items() if v>0}
    chosen[r]=next(pk for pk in pattern_data[r] if pattern_data[pk]['counts']==counts)

def restrict(pair):
    red=deepcopy(inst)
    new_pd={}
    new_pprk={}
    for r in relays:
        keep=pattern_data[r] if r in pair else [chosen[r]]
        new_pd[r]=keep
        for pk in keep:
            new_pd[pk]=pattern_data[pk]
            for (p,k),amt in pattern_data[pk]['counts'].items():
                new_pprk.setdefault((r,p,k),[]).append((pk,amt))
    red['pattern_data']=new_pd
    red['patterns_by_prk']=new_pprk
    return red
for pair in [('R_NORTH_PASS','R_EAST_PLATEAU'),('R_NORTH_PASS','R_URBAN_CORE'),('R_COAST_LINK','R_EAST_PLATEAU'),('R_PINE_CREEK','R_COAST_LINK')]:
    red=restrict(pair)
    model,xv,lv,mp=build_model(red,20)
    for r in relays:
        if r not in pair:
            model.addCons(lv[chosen[r]] == 1)
    model.addCons(sum(red['scenario_data'][s]['scenario_probability']*mp[s] for s in red['scenarios']) >= inc_obj + 1e-6)
    model.optimize()
    print(pair, 'status',model.getStatus(),'nsol',model.getNSols(),'time',model.getSolvingTime(),'bound',model.getDualbound(), 'obj', (model.getObjVal() if model.getNSols() else None))
